Document 003: 10月FOMC会议点评：再探沃什底色

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月FOMC会议点评：再探沃什底色.pdf
Report source: 国信证券
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

事项：
美东时间 10 月 28日，美联储宣布将联邦基金利率目标区间维持在 3.75%—4.00%不变。
评论：
10月 FOMC如期暂停加息，会议基调中性偏鹰。美东时间 10月 28 日，美联储以 10比 2 将联邦基金利率目
标区间维持在 3.75%-4.00%，库克和哈马克主张加息 25BP。声明将经济扩张速度由“稳健”（solid）上
调为“强劲”（strong），同时强调积累更多信息有助于确定适当政策立场。
议息会议后，市场对 12月加息概率定价并未改变，但对 2027年末政策利率的预期小幅下移，表明本次暂
停主要缓和了市场对后续累计收紧幅度的担忧，尚未改变年内再加息一次的主流判断。因此，市场对会议
作出了边际偏鸽的反应。短端美债受益、曲线趋陡，两年期美债收益率下行 6.2BP，10 年和 30 年期分别
上行 0.6BP、2.2BP。美元指数下跌约 0.24%，伦敦现货黄金上涨约 0.86%，标普 500 和纳斯达克分别上涨
约 0.08%和 0.27%。我们认为，10月 FOMC是沃什政策哲学的试金石，暂停逻辑与后续政策安排体现其对增

长和通胀的权衡。
 政策“试金石”：从暂停的理由，观察沃什的政策哲学
我们认为，10 月 FOMC 为观察沃什的政策哲学提供了一个窗口。当就业（非农偏弱）与通胀（通胀仍高）
指向不同方向时、再叠加中期选举前夕，他选择如何行动、又如何解释，更能体现其政策底色。
暂停行动、保持鹰派底色是联储当下可行性最高、代价最小的一条路。沃什虽然释放鹰派信号，但政策执
行仍带有偏鸽倾向。声明以“积累更多信息”（the accumulation of more information）解释暂停。但
高油价叠加去年四季度基数走低，预计 10月乃至 Q4 通胀仍将高位运行。因此，沃什的选择有两种解释：
①确实需要等待，但重点在于弱非农是否延续、AI 能够承受多少收紧，权衡的是进一步加息的代价；②底
色倾向暂停，“等待信息”只是保留政策余地的说法或接口，其中也不乏中期选举前求稳的考虑。前者说
明他仍顾及增长的代价，市场不必担心其不计代价地抗通胀；后者若获验证，则意味着其借经济理由维持
独立决策的表象，实际在一定程度上被政治裹挟，底色是“鹰嘴鸽”。

现有表态更支持第一种解释。沃什希望先确认利率对 AI 投资的约束效果，避免追加不必要的紧缩。在新
闻发布会上，他质疑 AI 投资“无惧加息”的说法，并将部分信用利差走阔、投资者更加挑剔视为政策起
效的迹象。我们判断，后续观察政策应当考虑新变量——AI产业链的利率敏感性。当前科技巨头现金流可
能面临压力，依赖外部融资的数据中心和基础设施企业仍会承压。若适度收紧便能约束 AI 投资，联储就
不必通过更大幅度加息。若适度加息后，AI 产业链仍健康且价格压力仍不降温，继续等待的必要性会下降。

### Page 2

 政策安排：收紧有所节制，沟通框架调整
本次会议还明确了资产负债表调整与议息会议安排的变化，为判断后续货币政策提供了新线索。
一是资产负债表调整短期内不会出现，近期收紧仍以利率工具为主。沃什表示，工作组建议预计于 2027
年一季度初提交 FOMC，相关调整不太会对本轮政策周期产生实质影响。我们认为，这降低了近期缩表
（quantitative tightening, QT）推进的预期。在长端利率高企、企业融资成本已经上升的背景下，暂
不叠加缩表，有助于控制金融压力，也为评估前期加息效果保留空间。
二是减少例行利率决策次数与淡化前瞻指引相互配合。每年八次会议总数不变，但 2027 年拟有一次、2028
年拟有两次转为战略讨论，预计不进行利率投票、不发布政策声明。结合沃什对前瞻指引的保留态度，我
们认为，这一调整有助于减少市场围绕例行决议反复博弈，为政策效果的观察留出更长窗口，缓和弱化指
引带来的预期扰动。后续政策路径的判断，将更加依赖经济数据及联储决策标准的一致性。
 往后看：加息仍有空间，节奏取决于政策传导

我们维持 12月加息 25BP、2027年另加 1-2 次的基准判断。本次暂停并未改变联储将通胀带回 2%的目标，
重点在于评估进一步收紧的必要性。我们认为，后续加息幅度取决于两股力量。能源涨价是否向核心分项
扩散，以及融资成本上升能否约束 AI投资与其他需求。
对市场而言，政策节奏缓和有利于短端和估值修复，长端持续回落仍需通胀改善配合。短端美债对加息路
径下修的反应更直接，因此直接受益。长端则同时受到增长、通胀补偿与债券供需的影响，暂停加息本身
不足以推动收益率持续下行。
后续判断政策路径，应同时比较通胀持续与收紧过度两侧的代价，避免由单月数据线性外推。价格端看核
心动能与涨价广度是否同步变化，传导端看融资收紧是否真正影响 AI 投资，就业端看短期波动是否演变
为持续走弱。三组证据共同决定联储继续加息的必要性与承受空间，也是检验沃什政策取舍的依据。

前次投資建議：2026.07.29： 
「買進」 ，目標價: $165

報告日期：2026.10.28 研 究 員：王穎

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 基於 eMMC 及 NOR 強勁需求，然漲價力道有限，調降為「逢低買進」投資評等，目標價
120 元(4.1X 2027(F)PER)潛在上漲空間 16.5%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/10/27 收盤價： 103.00 ➢ 26Q3 營收 242.37 億，QoQ+26.7%，YoY+195.1%，毛
利率 70.5%，QoQ+6.1ppts，YOY+57.0ppts，營業費用
41.53 億，營業淨利 129.3 億，業外收入 0.65 億，稅後
淨利 109.51 億，EPS 5.52元。每股淨值 36.95。營收及
毛利率成長主要受惠 NAND、eMMC、NOR FLASH等產
品 ASP漲價。 
➢ 展望 26Q4預期 NAND、eMMC、NOR FLASH等產品合
約價將持續漲價，然漲價幅度將較 26H1放緩且目前稼動
率已高出貨量於新增產能擴出前成長有限。因此兆豐預估
營收 280.66 億元，QoQ+15.8%，YoY+263.1%，毛利率
74.6%，EPS 6.93元。 
➢ 展望 2026-2027 年，受惠原廠減產趨勢導致 eMMC 需求
強勁，車規 eMMC 預期於 2027 年開始小量出貨，2028
年放量，增添公司營運動能，且 NOR FLASH 受惠 AI帶
動，需求強勁然後續價格漲幅有限，因應 NAND及 NOR 
FLASH 客戶強勁需求，預期 27H2 年新產能開出，將再
增強公司擴力表現。 因此兆豐預估 2026/2027 營收
818.97/1229.03 億元， YoY+183.6/50.1% ，毛利率  
66.7/74.1%，稅後淨利 342.1/582.8 億元， EPS 為
17.25/29.39元。

目標價 120 元(4.1 x 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 16.5%。

營 運 分 析

旺宏為全球 NOR Flash領導廠商 
旺宏是全球領先的 NOR Flash 製造商，提供涵蓋廣泛規格和容量的ROM唯
讀記憶體、NOR型及 NAND型快閃記憶體解決方案。目前公司的產能包括8
吋晶圓產能為每月 45-48 千片，12 吋晶圓產能為每月 20 千片，而 ROM 主
要透過外購再進行加工。公司還提供ROM、NOR型快閃記憶體及 NAND型
快閃記憶體的解決方案，並從事次微米邏輯製程、高壓 CMOS 及 BCD製程
的晶圓代工服務，包括嵌入式非揮發性記憶體的 BCD 及邏輯製程。公司的
廠房位於新竹科學園區，包含生產8吋晶圓的晶圓二廠、12吋晶圓的晶圓五
廠，以及產品測試廠。26Q3產品組合：NAND 47%、NOR 46%、ROM 5%、
FBG 2%。

26Q3  NOR、eMMC需求維持強勁 
26Q3 營收 242.37 億， QoQ+26.7%，YoY+195.1%，毛利率 70.5%，
QoQ+6.1ppts，YOY+57.0ppts，營業費用 41.53億，營業淨利129.3 億，業
外收入 0.65 億，稅後淨利 109.51 億，EPS 5.52 元。每股淨值 36.95。營收
及毛利率成長主要受惠 NAND、eMMC、NOR FLASH等產品 ASP 漲價。

圖二、Server 相關產品需求較佳 
             旺宏 26Q3 NOR產品組合

資料來源：旺宏；兆豐投顧整理

26Q4 eMMC、NOR FLASH需求維持強勁 
展望 26Q4 預期 NAND、eMMC、NOR FLASH 等產品合約價將持續漲價，
然漲價幅度將較 26H1 放緩且目前稼動率已高出貨量於新增產能擴出前成長
有限。因此兆豐預估營收 280.66 億元，QoQ+15.8%，YoY+263.1%，毛利
率 74.6%，EPS 6.93元。

26Q3 NOR、eMMC
需求強勁帶動毛利率
上升。 
26Q4 eMMC、NOR 
FLASH價格持續上
漲，但漲幅放緩。

個股報告 
圖三、26Q4產品漲價有利毛利提升 
旺宏各季營收及獲利走勢預估(單位：百萬)

資料來源：旺宏；兆豐投顧預估

NOR Flash 需求穩步上升，車用與伺服器市場成為成長主力 
整體 NOR Flash 產品將持續成長，特別是在車用和伺服器應用方面。隨著汽
車電子化和自動駕駛技術的發展，汽車行業對高可靠性、耐高溫的半導體需
求不斷增加，有利 NOR Flash 在車用市場用量持續增加。此外，伺服器市場
的需求也隨著數據中心的擴展而逐步上升。NOR Flash的快速響應和高可靠
性特性使其非常適合用於伺服器中的關鍵啟動代碼和固件應用。預期旺宏車
規 eMMC 預期於 2027 年開始小量出貨，2028 年放量，增添公司營運動能。

eMMC需求相對強勁，成獲利回溫關鍵 
展望 2026-2027 年，受惠原廠減產趨勢導致 eMMC 需求強勁，車規 eMMC
預期於 2027 年開始小量出貨，2028 年放量，增添公司營運動能，且 NOR 
FLASH 受惠 AI 帶動，需求強勁然後續價格漲幅有限，因應 NAND 及 NOR 
FLASH客戶強勁需求，預期 27H2 年新產能開出，將再增強公司擴力表現。
因此兆豐預估 2026/2027 營收 818.97/1229.03 億元，YoY+183.6/50.1%，
毛利率 66.7/74.1%，稅後淨利 342.1/582.8 億元，EPS 為 17.25/29.39 元。

記憶體持續供不應求，然漲價力道放緩，調降為「逢低買進」投資評等 
旺宏目前位於 3.5X 27(F)PER)，基於 eMMC 及 NOR強勁需求，然漲價力道
有限，調降為「逢低買進」投資評等，目標價 120 元(4.1 x 2 7(F)PER)，潛
在上漲空間 16.5%。

長線來看，車用與伺
服器應用成為 NOR 
Flash成長動能 
漲價力道有限，調降
為「逢低買進」投資
評等,目標價 120元。 
長期看好 NOR 及
eMMC 產品線持續成
長

### Page 1

M
 Update
UMC  | Asia Pacific
  Near term in line but larger 
long-term AI opportunity; stay 
OW
Larger AI opportunity into 2029: The company raised 2029 AI-related revenue 
guidance from >US$1bn to 25% of total revenue. Assuming ~15% YoY growth on our 
2028e revenue (c. US$14bn), 2029 pro forma AI revenue could reach ~US$4bn (25% 
of ~US$16bn) –  well above the prior US$1bn. Also, newly announced 12-inch silicon 
photonics capacity of 18kwpm could generate at least US$1.7–1.8bn assuming ~US
$8k wafer ASP – not to mention newly announced capacity of another 22kwpm for 
DTC/silicon capacitors and 20kwpm for Teflon modulators.
2027 price hike plan in line with our estimate: The company guided for a 10% 
improvement in 2027 blended wafer ASP. Excluding better product mix, like-for-like 
wafer ASP would rise 5–10%, which is in line with our estimate but slightly below 
the buy-side bogey of 10-15%. 
Weak consumer could be covered by other non-AI demand: UMC does  see some 
weakness showing up in PCs and midrange/low-end smartphones, but 
communications,  automotive, and industrial demand remains robust.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 6

Chroma ATE (2360.TW)
30 October 2026 Citi Research
6
Chroma ATE
Company description
Chroma ATE is a leading supplier of testing and instrumentation equipment,
automated testing systems (ATS), and turnkey solutions. Its products are
used for testing power electronics, semiconductor, IC, EV component,
display, and other electronics applications.
Investment strategy
We rate Chroma Buy as we expect structural and robust growth ahead, driven
by: 1) strong power tester demand for AI data center/servers; 2) expanding SLT

(system level testing) demand driven by new generation model launch and
new customers ramp-up; 3) strong metrology growth from increasing foundry
capex investment in advanced packaging; and 4) solid silicon photonics
equipment demand, with CPO a key incremental upside.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$2,900 is based on 40x 2027E P/E, at close to 1x PEG.
Chroma shares have re-rated above its historical range as its growth outlook
is now strongly tied to AI infrastructure build out. We estimate 60%+ of its

revenue are now AI-related, with strong growth across product segments
including power testers, SLT, metrology, and photonics. Chroma has a
commanding market share in most of these segments and well positioned to
capitalized on the global AI expansion.
Risks
Downside risks to achieving our target price include: 1) softer AI/CPO growth
expectation due to changes in capex outlook or production bottleneck; 2)
fewer SLT needed as a result of shortened cycle time as GPU yield rate

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

M
Idea
Further upside surprise: In our recent CPO report, we raised WIN Semi's revenue
forecasts  to reflect stronger demand for satellite communications RF, driven by
major customer SpaceX (SPCX; covered by Adam Jonas). However, we
underestimated the favorable pricing and margin, as evidenced by the 28 Oct   3Q26
results. Reflecting the much higher gross margin, we raise our 2026, 2027 and 2028
EPS by 35%, 28% and 17%, respectively, and raise our price target 17% to  NT$700  to

capture the long-term upside from both new businesses - space communications
and AI optical networking laser.
3Q26 margin beat: Core margin reached 40.7% in 3Q26, well above our original
forecast of 29%. The company indicated space communications revenue already
represents 40% of its infrastructure business, accounting for 35-40% of total
revenue. This implies the space-related revenue is tracking at c.15% of total revenue
in 3Q. Despite a relatively  small portion of revenue, we estimate the business could

lift 3Q26 margin by 5% (half of 3Q26 increase), implying the space communications
margin could be >60%, well above our and Street expectations. V3 satellite
development will be a key growth driver into 2027. See Exhibit 1  for our analysis.
4Q26 revenue guidance looks solid: Smartphone and WiFi revenue remain stable
despite weak China smartphone demand: Win Semi now has higher exposure to
Apple iPhone through high-band PA (Broadcom) and WiFi (Murata). As such,

revenue can still grow single digit QoQ in 4Q26, despite recent weakness in China
smartphone demand. Please see Exhibit 3  and Exhibit 4    for  our earnings review.
Upgrade to OW: The stock trades at 25x our new 2028 EPS. Given the significant
revenue contribution from space communications and AI lasers, we think our
implied target multiple of 29x 2028 EPS should be justified. Win Semi historically
traded at 40x PE during the smartphone 3D sensing product cycle.

2
Surprising Margin Expansion from Optical/Space Businesses
WIN Semi   could benefit from PD growth and potential LD outsourcing
by late 2027
In our previous WIN Semi report, we argued that Lumentum (LITE.O, covered by Meta
Marshall) was unlikely to outsource CW lasers or EML production to WIN Semi, a key
pillar of our earlier UW rating. However, our recent  checks with WIN Semi management
suggest that WIN Semi and its major customer, we assume it's Broadcom (AVGO, covered

by Joseph Moore), have secured sufficient InP substrate supply for WIN Semi's PD
production, potentially supporting substantial growth for the PD (photo diode) business
in 2H26. We estimate PD could account for >10% of WIN Semi's total revenue. Our recent
checks from the supply chain also suggest that WIN Semi's largest PD customer  could
outsource LD (laser diode) production to WIN Semi.
We also see the potential for even stronger revenue growth from WIN Semi's aerospace

business into 2027, especially from the ground station and satellite business. With
SpaceX's  launch of Starship and Starlink V3 satellite, we expect WIN Semi's content value
per satellite   to increase significantly from Starlink v2 to v3, with potential upside from
future DTD (direct to device) satellites. The increase in content value comes not only from
higher RF chips volumes, but also from the migration from GaAs to GaN. Based on our
industry checks, the ASP for 4-inch GaN could be US$9k, compared with ~US$2.5-3k for 6-

inch GaAs. We therefore upgrade our rating from EW to OW with the strong gross margin
performance, which we believe to be >60%.
Where we could be wrong with our OW call: We think the largest near-term swing factor
for WIN Semi is the aerospace business, which is relatively difficult to track given the
inherent risk of delays or failures in satellite launches. However, on September 28, 2026,
SpaceX successfully launched the Starship with Starlink V3 satellite. If SpaceX decides to

speed up the satellite constellation, there also could be upside to our forecasts.
All of our satellite launch numbers are based on Adam Jonas's SpaceX forecasts. Please
see his latest report: SpaceX: SpaceX/Tesla and SI's Next Act (23 Oct 2026)

3Q26 earnings and 4Q26 guidance review - margin and earnings beat on

strong optical and aerospace demand
WIN Semi delivered a solid 3Q26 performance, with margins and earnings exceeding
expectations, supported by robust demand across its optical and aerospace businesses.
The gross margin beat MSe and consensus by 7-8ppts.  Strength in optical applications,
driven by continued investment in AI-related optical connectivity and high-speed data
transmission, alongside resilient aerospace demand, supported a favorable product mix

and improved manufacturing efficiency.
The higher contribution from these relatively high-value-added segments lifted gross
margins above expectations, driving positive operating leverage and stronger-than-
anticipated earnings. Looking ahead, we expect sustained momentum in optical
communications and aerospace applications to remain key drivers of WIN Semi's
profitability.

We raise our 2026-28 EPS estimates by 17-35%:  We raise our 2026-28 margin forecasts
due to surprisingly strong margin performance from the optical and aerospace businesses,
and we expect the revenue exposure from these segments to continue growing. Besides,
the 3Q26 non-op investment gain is also larger than we expected, leading to a larger 2026
earnings revision.

WIN Semi: Valuation Methodology
Our price target (base-case value) increases from NT$600 to NT$700: This also factors
in our EPS forecast changes  for 2025 to 2028.
We continue to derive our price target from a residual income model as we believe it best
presents the value of the company. All of our other key assumptions are unchanged,
including a cost of equity of 8.0% (beta of 1.0, equity risk premium of 6% and risk-free

rate of 2%), intermediate growth rate assumption of 15% and terminal growth rate of
4.0%.
Our bull-case value  increases  from NT$900 to NT$1050 and our bear-case value from NT
$300 to NT$350, implying 44x and 14x 2028e EPS, respectively.

MS Rating
▪ Relatively strong iPhone demand (vs.
Android) appears likely in the near term, but
smartphone growth is mature compared to

AI demand.
▪ We expect to see more GaAs wafer
consumption as we migrate to the WiFi 7
era, as WiFi 7 has three bands (2.4 GHz, 5
GHz and 6 GHz) vs. WiFi 6 at just two (2.4
GHz, 5 GHz).
▪ GaN penetration in satellite business could
lift the wafer ASP.
▪ We think optical transceivers and robotics
may become future drivers, as WIN Semi has
the technology to enable products like EML,
CW-DFB and DML.
▪ We are OW given the strong margin
improvement from the new business.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

 投資評價與建議 
我們認為高階封裝業務進度如期，且為明年公司重點布局業務，整體狀況與原
先預期差異不大；我們給予公司18倍PE，為近期股價修正前之平均評價水準。 
 重點評論分析 
估4Q26營收維持高檔：當前公司封裝、測試產能利用率均達90%以上，推估
營收動能包含：(1)記憶體需求維持高檔，新業務TSV DDR5開始量產，另公司
持續因應記憶體報價上漲 ， 適時反映於封測報價 ； (2)邏輯業務中 ， FCBGA需求
維持穩健且公司將於本季導入更大尺寸封裝 ， 子公司超豐Flip Chip月產能於年
底擴充至2.3億顆，至於Tera Probe/Terapower除了有AI/MML需求支撐外，
⾞用需求回溫亦帶來成⻑動能。估4Q26營收季增5.8%至268億元，毛利率
24.1%，稅後EPS 3.8元。 
高階封裝業務逐步發酵、封測報價議價能力強勁支撐2027年成⻑動能 ：展望
2027年，基於：(1)DRAM市場供不應求至少延續至 2027年，機構估2027年
DRAM報價續漲但漲幅收斂，推估將持續支撐漲價成本之能力；(2)邏輯業務
中，封測產業產能利用率維持高檔，持續支撐公司議價能力，而公司高階封裝
比重成⻑帶動產品組合持續轉佳，如FOPLP明年有望開出3k月產能，子公司
Tera Probe/Terapower持續搶攻AI測試業務，另Terapower新竹新廠將於明
年貢獻營收。預估2027年營收年增22.5%至1,182.4億元，稅後EPS 18元。 
 投資風險 
FOPLP進展不如預期。

| 營運概況  
3Q26本業表現大致符合預期：力成3Q26營收季增9.6%至253.3億元，毛利率24%，稅後EPS 3.44元，其中本業表
現大致符合預期，營收動能來自於記憶體需求維持穩健，子公司超豐價格調漲和客⼾需求成⻑，T e r a  
Probe/Terapower持續受惠AI/MML、HPC封測訂單支撐，而獲利方面則有價格調漲、高產能利用率(力成3Q26封
裝、測試產能利用率分別為85~90%、85%)之挹注，帶動毛利率季增2.3ppts。 
估4Q26營收維持高檔：當前公司封裝、測試產能利用率均達90%以上，推估4Q26營收動能包含：(1)記憶體業務需
求維持高檔，其中企業級SSD持續受惠於AI伺服器需求，另TSV DDR5開始量產，估月營收貢獻約1~2億元，價格
方面，公司持續因應記憶體報價上漲並適時反映於封測報價；(2)邏輯業務中，FOPLP驗證進度如期推進，FCBGA
需求維持穩健且公司將於本季導入更大尺寸封裝(78mm*88mm)，子公司超豐Flip Chip月產能於年底擴充至2.3億
顆 ， 另將部分不具成本優勢之產品轉移至菲律賓廠量產 ， 至於Tera Probe/Terapower除了有AI/MML需求支撐外 ，
⾞用需求回溫亦帶來成⻑動能。我們估4Q26營收季增5.8%至268億元，毛利率24.1%，稅後EPS 3.8元。 
高階封裝業務逐步發酵、封測報價議價能力強勁支撐2027年成⻑動能：展望2027年，基於：(1)DRAM市場供不應
求至少延續至2027年，機構估2027年DRAM報價續漲但漲幅收斂，推估將持續支撐漲價成本之能力，至於NAND
市況據機構預估明年仍有個位數的缺口或趨於平衡，僅有企業級SSD需求仍維持強勁；(2)邏輯業務則為明年最大成
⻑動能，基於整體產業產能利用率維持高檔，支撐公司議價能力，加上高階封裝比重成⻑帶動產品組合持續轉佳，
包含FOPLP明年有望開出3k月產能，推估應用為AMD之電競晶片，子公司Tera Probe/Terapower除了既有業務持
續成⻑外，也積極搶攻美系客⼾下⼀代AI ASIC測試商機，另Terapower新竹新廠也將於明年開始貢獻營收(估月營
收貢獻約2~3億元)。預估2027年營收年增22.5%至1,182.4億元，毛利率24.5%，稅後EPS 18元。

陳奕均 fion.chen@sinopac.com

泰合 (6467 TT) 
開花結果在即

永豐觀點 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議買進：泰合 10/20 上市掛牌，掛牌價105.6 元。由於(1) 泰合開發之口溶
膜劑型藥物有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點下，市場競爭力足；(2) 抗血栓口
溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，2027 年在授權與上市權利金開始認列下 ，營運轉
虧為盈且呈大跳躍狀；(3) 由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至 2028 年 4
月後才可上市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜
分市場。且因 BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其
獨特的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的價折壓力相對輕微，建議買進，目
標價 170 元( 18 X 2027 EPS )。

營運現況與分析 
公司簡介：泰合生技成立於 2010 年，專注開發 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥與特殊學名
藥，核心技術為自主研發的 TDS 透皮／透黏膜傳輸平台，將現有口服藥品成分改
造為透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型 ，提供方便使用且更安全的新劑型藥物。 產品開發
上，505(b)(2)部分，治療抗血栓口溶膜貼片(每日兩片)BEQIA 靜待歐美取證中外，
其餘則仍在臨床階段中，困難學名藥部分，化療止吐口溶膜2018 年日本取證銷售
中外，其餘四項藥品則在進行洽談授權合作。 
圖一：泰合產品線開發時程

資料來源：泰合；永豐投顧研究部整理，Oct. 2026

### Page 2

2

口溶膜劑型藥物能有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點 ：傳統口服錠劑藥物必須經
過食道、進入胃腸道消化，再經由肝臟代謝後才進入血液循環，泰合開發之口溶膜
劑型藥物， 可讓藥膜在接觸舌頭或口腔黏膜數秒內快速溶解 ， 病患完全不需飲水即
可完成給藥， 且部份藥物分子能直接透過口腔黏膜上皮細胞微血管吸收， 直接進入
全身血液循環， 不僅能提高藥物生體可用率， 還能減少胃酸對藥物的破壞與對胃腸
道的刺激下，有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點，提高患者服藥順從性。 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，提前卡位下，營運動能可期：泰合自主
開發的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥 BEQIA， 預計4Q26 取得歐盟藥證 、2Q27 獲美國
FDA 核准上市。目前該藥原廠在美國市場年銷售額>200 億美元，相較原廠口服錠
劑，泰合 BEQIA 具備三大競爭優勢：不需飲水（服藥便利）、解決吞嚥困難（減
輕照顧端壓力），以及攜帶與給藥便利（優化長照與醫療機構給藥流程）。 
市場競爭方面，由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至2028 年 4 月後才可上
市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜分市場。此
外，由於BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其獨特
的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的折價壓力應相對輕微。 
泰合海外市場營銷預計將採授權分潤模式，研究部預估 1H27 歐美地區簽約金應
有機會各達 500~1000 萬美金外，隨美國地區 2Q27 上市、通路鋪設及醫保陸續
准入，Peak sales 市佔率 2-3%(預期將落於 2029-30 年)、銷售分潤 12-15%估算
下，稅後 EPS 貢獻將落於 10-20 元。 
財務預估：預估泰合 2026 年營收 0.1 億元，稅後虧損 1.89 億元，稅後 EPS 以掛
牌後股本 5.99 億元估算，稅後 EPS-3.15 元，2027 年預估歐美地區授權簽約金各
500 萬美金及 1000 萬美金 ， 及上市銷售後權利金認列下 ，預估營收將跳升至7.75
億元，稅後淨利 5.68 億元，稅後 EPS 9.49 元。

隨客製化需求持續成長、柬埔寨廠人力恢復至5,000人並開始協助
客戶回補庫存，帶動3Q26營收創下歷史新高，且受惠於營收規模
擴大、產能利用率提升及關稅返還挹注，淨利率有望創高。展望後
市，公司持續擴增墨西哥產能，員工人數已達1,800人，後續預計
每年增加約 500 人，以改善交期並滿足客製化窗簾訂單成長需求；
越南新廠預計 2027年初投產，第一期規劃員工約 1,000人，可望
進一步擴大客製化產品產能與市佔率。市場需求方面，儘管美國市
場面臨高通膨及消費降級壓力，但公司憑藉穩健的財務結構，客製
化產品與同業價差已擴大至 30%～40%，具價格競爭優勢，有助
於提升市佔率及整體訂單量，預期維持 mid-single digit成長；固
定尺寸則透過新品推出，支撐營收維持 low-single digit成長；歐
洲市場方面，隨荷蘭、比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，可望延續
high-single digit 成長。獲利方面，受惠於關稅稅率降至 20%、
漲價及營收規模效益逐步顯現，預期毛利率將延續成長趨勢。綜合
以上，研究部預估 2026、2027 年 EPS 為 23.92、27.91 元，以
16倍本益比評價，給予目標價 450元，投資建議為買進。  
 近期投資評等  2Q26 毛利率創高，惟油價及運價上漲壓抑獲利表現 
2Q26 營收 79.27 億元，YoY+2.86%，QoQ+8.52%，稅後損
益 13.91 億元，YoY+20.67%，QoQ-15.57%，EPS 4.75 元，
低於研究部預期。第二季為傳統消費旺季，營運表現較第一季
明顯回升。區域營收方面，北美市場受到消費降級影響，加上
柬埔寨廠人力短缺，營收YoY-2.1%；非美市場則受惠於荷蘭、
比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，營收 YoY+19.8%，成為主
要成長動能。獲利方面，2Q26 毛利率 59.95%，YoY+0.7ppt，
QoQ+0.9ppt，受惠於客制化及歐洲市場營收比重持續提升，
帶動毛利率創歷史新高，惟油價及燃油附加費急漲，使費用率
升至 32.7%，抵銷部分毛利率改善效益，壓抑整體獲利表現。

 柬埔寨廠產能恢復，固定尺寸產品開始回補庫存 
展望 3Q26，9M26 營收 26.62 億元 ，YoY+13.5%，MoM-5.5%，
累計前 9 月營收 237.14 億元，YoY+6.3%，優於研究部預期。
第三季營收創歷史新高，台幣營收 YoY+16%、美金營收
YoY+9%，主要受惠於客制化需求持續成長，且柬埔寨廠已補
足至 5,000 人，開始協助客戶補庫存。獲利方面，隨營收規模
擴大、產能利用率提升，加上關稅返還挹注，預估3Q26 毛利

現金股利(去年) 14.5 率將優於去年同期；儘管油價及燃油附加費仍高於去年同期，
但較第二季小幅回落，加上營收規模擴大有助於費用率下降，

YoY+14.44%，QoQ+40.42%，EPS 6.67 元。

 越南新廠預計 1Q27投產，挹注未來 3~4年營運成長 
公司客製化窗簾產能主要布局於中國及墨西哥，分別占整體產
能約 60%及 40%。中國廠員工人數約 4,000~4,500 人；墨西
哥則鎖定北美市場，截至 2026 年 9 月，舊廠與新廠合計員工
已達 1,800 人，預計逐年增加約 500 名員工，以改善交期問
題並因應市佔擴張需求，透過規模經濟及生產效率提升，進一
步優化整體獲利結構。柬埔寨廠則以固定尺寸窗簾為主，1H26
受缺工影響，出貨進度放緩， 6M26 起員工人數已由先前的
4,000 人回升至 5,000人，並開始協助客戶回補庫存至健康水
位。越南新廠預計於 2027 年初投產，聚焦客制化產品，第一
期規劃員工人數約 1,000 人，為中長期成長動能。

 同業價差擴大提升競爭力，歐洲市場成長動能延續 
儘管美國消費市場面臨高通膨、消費降級及關稅等壓力，但公
司憑藉穩健的財務結構，與同業的價差已擴大至30%～40%，
加上產品持續迭代更新，有助於提升市佔率及整體訂單量，預
估 2026~2027 年客製化業務可維持 mid-single digit成長；固
定尺寸則透過新品免打孔窗廉推出，提高平均售價，以維持
low-single digit 成長。歐洲市場方面，採線上直營電商模式，
專業人員到府量測與安裝服務，由於服務利潤率較高，已成為
主要獲利來源。目前已拓展至荷蘭、比利時、丹麥等市場，隨
覆蓋率快速提升，預期非美地區將延續 high-single digit 成
長。電動窗簾方面，價格為手動款的 2～2.5 倍，儘管目前佔
比仍為 low-single digit，但 CAGR 達 15%～20%以上，預期
未來營收占比有望提升至 10%，進一步帶動整體 ASP 及產品
組合優化。

 漲價效益與產品組合優化，支撐毛利率續創高 
原料成本方面，受美伊戰爭反覆升溫影響，PVC 價格維持 850
美元/噸的相對高檔，然公司近年持續調整配方並提高環保材
料使用比重，降低對 PVC 價格波動的依賴，以減輕短期原料

成本變動對獲利的衝擊。此外，公司已於 2M26 啟動漲價計

### Page 1

(005930) 
삼성전자 
2026.09.29

반도체 손인준 
6154 / sij@eugenefn.com

긴 호흡으로 
 투자의견 STRONG BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 560,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 270,000원(9/28)

 삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익이 103.6조원(+16%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 메모리 가격 등 전반적인 반도체 업황에 대한 기존 가정은 대체로 
유지했으나, 최근 원화 강세에 따라 원/달러 환율 가정을 1,440원에서 
1,400원으로 낮추고 MX 사업부의 적자 확대를 반영해 실적 추정치를 하
향 조정했다. 또한 2Q26 재고가 3Q26 매출로 인식되는 과정에서 발생하
는 추가 성과급 충당금 부담도 반영했다.  
 DS 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 104.0조원(+17%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 서버 DRAM과 eSSD 중심의 가격 인상이 이어지며, DRAM과 
NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +17%, +18%QoQ 상승할 것으로 추정한
다. 1cnm 수율 개선과 HBM4의 빠른 램프업도 실적 개선에 기여할 전망이
다. Foundry/LSI는 HBM4 베이스다이 생산 확대, Groq 3 LPU 양산 및 선
단 공정 가격 인상에 힘입어 하반기 매출 성장이 예상된다. 다만 성과급 충
당금과 감가상각비 부담으로 영업적자 축소 폭은 제한적일 것으로 보인다. 
 DX 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 -1.8조원(적자지속qoq)을 기록할 것으로 추
정한다. 삼성전자는 메모리 가격 급등에도 제품 가격 인상을 제한하며 시장 
점유율 확대에 주력하고 있다. 이러한 가격 정책과 갤럭시 Z 폴드8 등 신제
품 흥행에 힘입어 판매량은 당초 예상치를 상회할 것으로 보인다. 다만 원
가 상승분의 전가가 제한되면서 영업적자 폭은 크게 확대될 전망이다.  
 삼성전자의 4Q26 영업이익은 114.9조원(+11%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. DRAM과 NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +11%, +12%QoQ 상승
하며 견조한 흐름을 이어갈 것으로 보인다. 환율 가정 조정에 따라 2026년
과 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 각각 365.2조원(+735%yoy), 595.8조원
(+63%YoY)으로 낮췄다. 4Q26~1Q27 범용 DRAM·NAND 및 2027년 
HBM 가격 협상 환경은 공급업체에 유리하게 전개되고 있어, 향후 가격 가
정의 추가 상향 가능성이 높다고 판단한다.  
 극심한 DRAM 공급 부족으로 인한 HBM 적층 단수 하향 조정(base die 수
요 증가), Memory Wall에 대응하기 위한 3D Stacked DRAM·zHBM 도입 
논의 등은 파운드리의 기술적 가치를 부각시키는 요인이다. 파운드리의 수
익성 회복 속도는 완만할 전망이지만, 메모리와 파운드리를 연계한 턴키 비
즈니스의 시너지는 AI 반도체 시장 내 경쟁력을 높이고, 메모리 시장 점유
율 확대에도 기여할 것으로 보인다. 적극적인 메모리 가격 인상 정책으로 
업계 최고 수준의 Blended ASP도 장기간 유지할 것으로 예상한다. 
 이제 메모리 업종 주가는 업황의 지속 가능성을 확인하고 꾸준한 주주환원
을 반영하며, 중장기적으로 점진적인 상승세를 이어갈 것으로 판단한다. 삼
성전자는 메모리·파운드리를 아우르는 턴키 역량과 가격 정책에 기반한 실
적 차별화를 통해 피어 그룹 내 경쟁력을 높여갈 것으로 보인다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

Document 227: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한_07bab1f0

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한_07bab1f0.pdf

### Page 1

(034020) 
두산에너빌리티 
2026.10.30

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 80,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 56,700 원(10/29)

 [투자의견] 당사는 두산에너빌리티에 대해 목표주가를 80,000원(2027년 
BPS의 6.2배)으로 27% 하향하며, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 실적은 견조
한 회복을 보이고 있으나, 최근 GE Vernova(GEV) 등 글로벌 AI 및 에너지 
밸류체인 Peer 주가 조정을 반영해 멀티플을 하향 조정했다. 체코 시공 계
약 등 일부 노이즈에 의해 주가가 급락한 만큼 매수 기회라고 판단한다.  
 [3Q26 Review]  매출액 4.4조원(-5.8%qoq, +14.7%yoy), 영업이익 
2,612억원(-16.9%qoq, +90.6%yoy)을 기록해 당사 추정에 부합하는 무
난한 실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티 부문은 매출액 1.8조원, 영업이익 1,211
억원(영업이익률 6.6%, +2.3%p qoq)을 기록하며 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 
고수익 사업 믹스 개선으로 분기 수익성이 재차 개선됐다. 당기순이익은 
149억원(-93.4%qoq)에 그쳤으나, 원달러 환율 급락으로 발생한 환손실 
반영 때문으로 이익 체력과는 무관하다고 판단한다. 3Q26 미국 빅테크 데
이터센터향 가스터빈 첫 출하를 달성했으며, 수주잔고는 에너빌리티 부문 
기준 26.6조원(+70.9%yoy)으로 사상 최대치를 경신했다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 4.8조원, 영업이익 2,640억원으로 유사한 실적 
흐름을 전망한다. 4Q 가스터빈 인도와 체코 두코바니 원전 진행률 반영으로 
에너빌리티 부문 외형 성장이 기대된다. 3Q26 별도 기준 영업이익률이 
8.5%까지 개선된 만큼 에너빌리티 부문의 분기 실적 개선 추세는 유효하다
고 판단한다. 연간 수주 목표 13.3조원 달성을 위한 잔여 수주(3.3조원 +α)
가 예정되어 있으며, 대형원전을 제외하더라도 가이던스 달성은 무난할 것
이라 판단한다. 세부적으로는 SMR 1조원(계약 조건 합의 완료 단계로 연내 
체결 가능), 신재생 7,000억원(해상풍력 기자재·EPC 2건 협의 완료 단계), 
해외 복합 EPC 5,000억원(국내 1건, 해외 2건)이 기대된다. 
 [가스터빈] 3Q26 누적 가스터빈 13기·스팀터빈 11기·LTSA 6건을 달성하
며 연간 10기 이상 수주·수행 역량이 안정적으로 유지되고 있다. 2030년 
슬롯 예약 물량의 본계약 전환은 2027년, 2031년 슬롯은 2028년으로 예
상되며, 수주잔고 증가가 기대된다. 가스터빈 수요 증가를 감안하면 추가 캐
파 증설 결정이 이르면 연내 이루어질 전망이다. 
 [원전] 10월 NuScale 1개 모듈 제작 계약을 체결했으며, PPA 지연 여부와 
무관하게 완결된 계약으로 실적에 반영될 전망이다. X-energy는 연내 기자
재 및 부품의 후속 계약을 추진 중이다. TerraPower 주기기 3종 중 2종 계
약이 완료되어 제작에 착수했으며, Rolls-Royce SMR·i-SMR 등 파트너십 
확대도 지속되고 있다. 이외에 대미투자 관련 기자재 발주는 미국 부지 확정 
및 한국 의회 승인 절차가 남아있어 내년 발주가 기대된다. 기존 웨스팅하우
스 AP1000 Vogtle 계약 대비 업무 범위 확대(원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 
품목) 가능성이 있어 긍정적으로 평가한다.

### Page 2

2_ Eugene Research Center 
설명회 Q&A 
Q1. 4Q26 SMR 수주 목표 약 1 조원의 실현 가능성 
- 고객사와 GTC 등 주요 계약 조건에 대한 합의를 대부분 완료하고 계약 체결 대기 중 
- 연내 계약 가능성이 있어 수주 목표는 의미 있는 수준

Q2. NuScale 모듈 1 기 제작 계약의 PP A 지연 영향 
- 완결된 제작 계약으로 PP A 체결이 지연되더라도 해당 모듈의 제작 및 진행에는 문제없음

Q3. AP1000 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 회계 처리 및 사업 영향 
- DOE·DOC 및 한국 정부 재원을 활용해 EPC 계약·착공 지연과 무관하게 기자재 제작 가능 
- 프로젝트 중단 시 웨스팅하우스의 다른 프로젝트로 기자재 전용 가능성 검토

Q4. 체코 원전 컨소시엄 변경 시 참여 및 사업 계획 
- 현지 지점 운영과 시공인력 상주로 시공 환경 자료 확보 
- 한수원과 초기 인허가·설계에 참여하고, 주기기 공급·설치 역량을 바탕으로 경쟁력 확보 기대

Q5. 체코 원전 EPC 사업의 수익성 확보 가능성 
- 한수원 가이드에 따라 수익성을 고려한 입찰 진행 예정

Q6. 웨스팅하우스 SPV 및 폴란드 AP1000 장주기 기자재 발주 진행 상황 
- DOE·DOC 프로젝트와 폴란드 AP1000 3기 기자재 조기 발주 협의 중 
- 연내 수주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 수주 추진

Q7. 3Q26 영업이익률 개선 요인 
- 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 사업 믹스 개선이 수익성에 기여 
- 제작 진행률과 마일스톤에 따라 이익 인식

### Page 3

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _3 
Q8. 4Q26 복합화력 수주 목표 5,000억원의 달성 가능성 
- 국내 1 건, 해외 2 건 협의 중이며 국내 프로젝트의 수주 가능성이 높은 상황 
- 대미 투자 물량은 기존 2031년까지의 예약계약 납기와 비교해 검토 중

Q9. 3Q26 에너빌리티부문 당기순손실 원인 
- 급격한 환율 하락에 따른 약 800억원의 환산손실이 주요 원인

Q10. 대미 투자 관련 기자재 발주 시점 
- 미국 부지 확정, 웨스팅하우스 사업제안 및 한국 정부 심사·국회 승인 절차 필요 
- 연말~내년 초 발주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 발주 추진

Q11. 대미 투자 프로젝트의 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 품목까지 공급 범위 확대를 위해 웨스팅하우스와 협상 중

Q12. 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 실적 영향 
- 기자재 출하 시 수금 조건이 충족되며 선발주 자금도 사전 확보 
- 보글·VC 서머 사례에서 기자재 대금을 회수해 프로젝트 지연에 따른 실적 영향은 제한적

Q13. 4Q26 신재생·풍력 수주 목표 약 7,000억원의 달성 가능성 
- 해상풍력 기자재·EPC 계약 및 유지보수 계약 추진 중 
- 주요 계약 협의가 상당 부분 완료돼 일정 지연이 없다면 연내 목표 달성 전망

Q14. 중형 가스터빈 개발 및 해외 진출 계획 
- 대형 가스터빈은 한국·미국 중심으로 추진하며 중동 고객사와도 협의 중 
- 휴스턴 서비스 허브를 기반으로 미국 데이터센터 시장 대응 
- 중형 가스터빈은 시제품 제작 및 시험 진행 후 2028년 하반기 출하 목표

### Page 4

두산에너빌리티

4_ Eugene Research Center 
Q15. NuScale 모듈 1 기 선발주 배경 
- 대규모 일괄 발주에 대비한 제작 경험 확보 및 납기 리스크 완화를 위해 사전 제작 추진

Q16. X-energy 연내 후속 계약 대상 품목 
- 증기발생기 전열관 및 주단 소재 등 장주기 품목의 사전 발주 추진

Q17. DOE 프로젝트의 장주기 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 웨스팅하우스 공급망 상황에 따라 추가 품목 협상 중 
- 과거 보글·VC 서머보다 넓은 공급 범위 기대

Q18. 웨스팅하우스 지분 투자와 기자재 계약 시점의 연계 가능성 
- 지분 투자는 내년, 기자재 계약은 연내 또는 내년 초 목표로 시점이 일치할지는 불확실

Q19. X-energy 장주기 기자재의 소재·부품 구분 
- 증기발생기 전열관은 부품, 주단 소재는 소재에 해당 
- 전열관은 공급 병목에 대비해 조기 발주하고 주단 소재는 설계 확정 후 제작 준비

Q20. 복합화력 주기기 수주의 기존·신규 고객 비중 
- 기존 고객과 추가 예약계약을 협의 중이며 신규 글로벌 고객과도 예약계약 협상 진행

Q21. 주요 SMR 사업 외 파트너십 확대 가능성 
- Rolls-Royce 사업 추진 및 GE Hitachi BWRX-300 주단 소재 예약계약 체결 
- i-SMR은 2035년 운전을 목표로 2028년 이전 발주가 필요해 관련 협의 진행

Q22. X-energy 전열관·주단 소재 수주 물량 규모 
- 다우케미컬 초도 사업은 80MW 원자로 4 개 모듈로 구성되며, 해당 4 개 모듈분 부품·소재 공급

### Page 5

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _5 
Q23. 중동 지정학적 불안정에 따른 프로젝트·물류 영향 
- 기존 프로젝트의 운송비 증가분을 발주처·개발사와 협의 중 
- 신규 계약에는 비용 보상 및 공기 지연 관련 조항 반영

Q24. 가스터빈 생산능력 증설 규모 및 시기 
- 현재 생산능력 증설 중이며 2031년 출하분까지 예약 접수 
- 시장 수요에 따라 추가 증설 검토

Q25. 2030년 가스터빈 예약 물량의 실제 계약 전환 시점 
- 2030년 출하 물량은 대부분 내년 실제 계약 여부 결정 전망 
- 2031년 출하 물량은 2028년경 계약 여부 결정 예상

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%)

ㆍBUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +15%이상 ~ +50%미만 94%

Document 255: AI最危险的交易或许不在科技股，而在美元

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI最危险的交易或许不在科技股，而在美元.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

德意志银⾏警告， AI 泡沫若破裂，冲击可能从美股蔓延⾄美元。外国投资者持有逾 20 万亿
美元美国股票，若股市、美元与美国实际利率同步下⾏，传统汇率对冲机制或将失效， AI
抛售潮可能演变为美元危机。
摘要：
当 AI 泡沫开始消退，最⼤的⻛险可能不是纳斯达克回调，⽽是美元崩塌。外国投资者持有逾 20
万亿美元的美国股票资产，⼀旦 AI 驱动的抛售引发股市与美元同步下跌，市场将⾯临远超技术性
修正的宏观冲击。
德意志银⾏警告， AI 抛售潮可能触发美国股市、货币对冲与美元之间的危险反馈循环。与以往科
技股回调不同，这⼀次美元的传统避险属性正受到根本质疑 —— 强美元未必能再充当外国投资
者的损失缓冲垫。
如果纳斯达克、美元指数（ DXY ）与美国实际利率三者同步下⾏，市场正在定价的将不仅是仓
位的技术性调整，⽽是对整个 AI 繁荣周期的系统性重估。这意味着下⼀轮 AI 抛售，有可能演变为
⼀场美元危机。
20 万亿美元的脆弱敞⼝
据德意志银⾏估算，外国投资者持有的美国股票资产中，约 90% 的价值来⾃累积的价格升值，

⽽⾮实际净购⼊。换⾔之，这⼀庞⼤头⼨⾼度依赖美国股市估值的持续维持。
更关键的是，外国股票资⾦流⼊⽬前已承担了美国经常账⼾⾚字的全部融资需求，美元因此与
股票市场周期深度绑定。⼀旦股市出现⼤幅调整，美元的资⾦来源将直接承压。
对冲机制在美国本⼟冲击下失效
过去⼗年，每当美国股市下跌，美元⾛强往往帮助外国投资者对冲了部分损失。但这⼀保护机
制有⼀个前提：冲击来⾃外部。若⻛险源头在美国本⾝，股市与美元可能同向下跌，对冲逻辑
随之⽡解。
StoneX 的数据显⽰， AI 情绪与美元之间的五⽇滚动相关系数⾼达 +0.97 ，⽽ 20 ⽇相关系数仅为
+0.31 。这意味着短期内两者已形成⾼度同步 ——AI 信⼼⼀旦动摇，美元将同步承压。
对于未对冲的外国投资者⽽⾔，股票和汇率的双重亏损将造成双重打击。即便他们不抛售股
票，单纯提⾼货币对冲⽐例的操作本⾝，也会形成额外的抛售美元压⼒，加剧汇率下⾏。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

实际利率是关键验证信号
德意志银⾏认为， AI 资本开⽀预期的急速膨胀，是推动美国股市与实际利率同步⾛⾼的重要驱动
⼒之⼀。因此，实际利率的⾛向将成为判断市场性质的关键信号。
若科技股、美元与美国实际利率三者同步下⾏，将表明投资者开始折现⼀个更疲弱的 AI 投资周
期 —— 这将把⼀次技术性股市修正，升级为更⼴泛的宏观重新定价。相反，若实际利率维持⾼
位，则调整更可能只是仓位层⾯的波动。
做空美元或成 AI 修正的隐性交易
⾯对 AI 潜在回调，最直观的交易是做空科技股。但德意志银⾏指出，不那么显眼、却可能更具威
⼒的交易是做空美元。
⼀旦以美国为中⼼的股票冲击成真，外国投资者将被迫重新审视其在这 20 万亿美元资产上的货
币对冲策略，传统的股票 - 美元正相关关系可能发⽣逆转。投资者需要同时盯紧纳斯达克、 DXY
与美国实际利率三个指标。若三者共同⾛低，市场所定价的⻛险将远⼤于⼀次普通的仓位出清
—— 下⼀轮 AI 抛售，或将以⼀场美元抛售收场。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 353: Lagarde’s comments point to a December ECB rate hike

Source PDF: all report pdf/Lagarde’s comments point to a December ECB rate hike.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Upside risks to inﬂation and downside risks to growth clearly
complicate the ECB's job. However, comments at today's press
conference suggest the ECB is set to hike again in December
Carsten Brzeski
ECB President Christine Lagarde addresses a press conference following
today's meeting
Rates on hold and Lagarde out. That’s the headline from today’s
ECB meeting. But look beyond that, and the message is more
nuanced: despite growing stagﬂationary pressures, the ECB still

looks set to hike rates again in December.
The decision to keep interest rates unchanged was not a surprise.
The main argument behind this decision is that there are currently
few knock-on eﬀects from higher headline inﬂation, while ﬁnancing
conditions, mainly bond yields, have become more restrictive.
A pause after two rate hikes this year was expected, even if macro
developments since the last meeting could have justiﬁed another
rate hike. Headline inﬂation increased again in September, and

### Page 2

most leading indicators point to continued resilience in the
eurozone economy. However, the surge in bond yields on the back
of the French crisis is already imposing a new restrictive monetary
policy stance. Or in other words, the bond market has taken over
the ECB’s job.
According to Christine Lagarde, risks to the eurozone’s growth
outlook remain tilted to the downside, while risks to the inﬂation
outlook are tilted to the upside. A combination of risks that clearly

complicates the ECB’s path ahead. This dilemma was underscored
by her observation that key developments since the September
meeting have been a further rise in energy prices and a worsening
of ﬁnancing conditions.
Despite these new complications, the ECB apparently debated a
rate hike today. However, the worsening of ﬁnancing conditions
took the option oﬀ the table.
Lagarde also emphasised the importance of indirect and second-
round eﬀects from higher energy prices. Currently, the ECB is not

seeing any additional second-round eﬀects, other than the ones
included in the September staﬀ projections.
ECB looks set to hike again in December
despite increasing stagﬂationary pressures
Looking ahead, there are three main parameters that, in our view,
will determine the ECB’s path going forward:
1. The length and intensity of the war in the Middle East. The
longer oil prices remain elevated, the higher the probability of
more rate hikes.
2. The knock-on eﬀects of higher energy prices. So far, there has

hardly been any pass-through of higher energy prices in the
rest of the economy. If this remains the case, the need for
further rate hikes drops. If it doesn’t, the possibility of rate hikes
rises.
3. France. Any solution to France's current ﬁscal woes could bring
some relief to bond markets and allow the ECB to continue
hiking rates. Without any solution, bond markets have taken

over the ECB’s role, making rate hikes less likely (and less
needed).
With today’s comments, another rate hike at the ECB’s December

meeting is clearly in the oﬃng. It would require a sharp worsening
of the growth outlook to probably prevent the ECB from hiking once
again.
In short, with downside risks to the growth outlook and upside risks
to the inﬂation outlook, the ECB seems to be increasingly
concerned about stagﬂationary forces, without calling them
stagﬂationary. Remember back in April, Lagarde called ‘stagﬂation’
a ‘ﬂashy term’.

Document 375: Persistent energy pressures have further to run (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Persistent energy pressures have further to run (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

MIDDLE EAST
BULLETIN
Persistent energy pressures have further to run
19 October 2026
Key points
• Shipping disruptions, depleted
inventories and elevated freight
costs are keeping physical crude
and fuel markets tight despite a

reported recovery in Gulf exports.
• Brent is forecast to average
US$101/bbl in Q4 before easing
to US$77/bbl in Q4 2027.
• Refined product markets are
expected to ease more slowly than
crude, with elevated gasoil cracks,
shipping and insurance costs slowing
declines in petrol and diesel prices.
• Under an escalation scenario,
renewed conflict and deeper supply
disruptions could push Brent towards
US$150/bbl in H1 2027, prolonging
elevated fuel and LNG prices.

• Under an early resolution scenario, a
durable US-Iran agreement accelerates
supply recovery, taking Brent towards
US$65/bbl by Q4 2027 and bringing
fuel and LNG prices down faster.
Q4 2026 forecasts:
Brent crude
US$101/bbl
Japanese LNG
US$24.5/MMBtu

### Page 2

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Low inventories and higher shipping costs will keep oil and
fuel prices elevated, with Brent futures forecast to average
US$101/bbl in Q4 before easing to US$77/bbl by Q4 2027
as supply recovers. Refined fuels and LNG prices should

ease more slowly. Risks are two-sided: escalation could
push Brent towards US$150/bbl, while an early US-Iran
agreement could see a faster normalisation in supply with
prices returning close to pre-conflict levels by end-2027.
This article was first published in the October Market Outlook .
Brent crude is back above US$100/bbl on fading confidence
in a lasting US-Iran agreement and intermittent attacks on
shipping. Buffers remain depleted, with the IEA estimating

global observed inventories were 507mb lower at the end of
August than at the start of the conflict.
This comes despite reported crude exports through the Strait of
Hormuz recovering to around 70% to 90% of pre-conflict levels
in late September. Saudi and UAE exports through the Yanbu and
Fujairah pipelines continue, with the East-West pipeline back to
normal after the early September attacks. Tanker crossings have
risen, although transponders remain switched off, leaving visible

transit at 1% to 4% of pre-conflict levels, while cargo transfers in
the Gulf of Oman have increased as an alternative route.
However, these workarounds are slower and more costly. Ship-
to-ship transfers can take 5 to 10 days, US escorts through
the Gulf via a route close to the Oman shoreline cause delays
while some tankers are taking longer routes. Fewer ships are
also available, with many redeployed to other markets. Higher
insurance premiums are adding to costs, with Gulf-to-China

tanker rates above US$0.9mn a day, around 15x higher than a
year ago. With prompt barrels (available for physical delivery)
scarce, the gap between physical and futures prices widened
again, with Dated Brent around US$136/bbl in early October
against US$101/bbl for front-month futures.
Oil and fuel prices push higher ...
Refined prices have risen more than crude
Refined product markets were already tight going into the
conflict. Reduced Gulf supply, exacerbated by Ukrainian strikes

on Russian refineries, has added to that tightness. Together
with higher shipping costs and rising Dated Brent prices,
Singapore Gasoil 10ppm (diesel) has roughly doubled since the
Strait closed, compared with around 40% for crude futures.
Consequently, Singapore gasoil crack spread widened
to an average of around US$36.1/bbl in early October,
from US$17.5/bbl across 2025. Alongside the Australian
government’s excise tax returning to normal, after it had been

halved, this has lifted national diesel and petrol prices to
around A$2.83/L and A$2.41/L.
Outlook
We do not see a lasting US-Iran agreement until at least
mid-2027. In the meantime, we assume there will not be a
return to full-scale military intervention. However, periodic
Iranian attacks on ships and facilities are expected to continue,
as will the US blockade of Iranian exports, keeping a supply
risk premium in place. We look for Brent futures to average

around US$101/bbl in Q4, then ease gradually as demand
from refiners and governments to rebuild stocks partly offsets
recovering supply. As the premium fades and the market
moves into surplus in late 2027, we forecast Brent at around
US$77/bbl in Q4 2027, easing towards US$70/bbl in 2028 as
Middle East production fully recovers.
Oil balance to gradually improve over 2027
Global oil supply is expected to rise by 4.0mb/d (4.0%) to
103.7mb/d in 2027, following a 6.1% fall in 2026. Most of

this reflects a gradual recovery in Gulf output, with Houthi
Sian Fenner, Head of Business & Industry Economics
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Luka Belobajdic, Economist

### Page 3

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
attacks on Red Sea shipping keeping Saudi production below
its pre-conflict level until H2 2027. Meanwhile, we look for UAE
production to continue to improve over 2027 and lift above
pre-conflict levels from 2028, as it is no longer constrained by

OPEC quotas. We do not expect a meaningful return of Iranian
oil until an agreement is reached, and damage sustained during
the conflict means production will take time to recover.
We expect global oil demand to be 103.7mb/d in 2026, 0.8mb/d
(0.7%) higher than in 2025, and to rise a further 1.9mb/d (1.8%)
to 105.6mb/d in 2027. Chinese crude imports will continue to
recover over the coming months as refiners restock ahead of
Lunar New Year, but we expect only a gradual return to pre-

conflict levels, as the electrification of transport limits the
recovery in Chinese demand. Globally, demand for diesel and jet
fuel recovers as transport and aviation activity picks up, although
some demand destruction and efficiency gains are expected
to be permanent. The conflict has also highlighted strategic
vulnerabilities, particularly across Asia, so we expect a push to
rebuild reserves adding around 0.5% to demand over 2027.
Fuel prices to ease more slowly than crude

Refined product supply is expected to recover more slowly
than crude, with Gulf refinery runs not returning to pre-war
levels until at least late 2027. Outside the Gulf, there has
been some constructive news. The US has lifted sanctions
on Russian diesel until April 2027, with Russia saying it will
supply more than 4Mt over the coming months, while China
has lifted its ban on refined product exports. However, we
are cautious about how much this will add to the market in

the short term. Much of the Russian supply is headed to the
US and will depend on Russia’s ability to restore refining and
export capacity, while doubts remain over the Russia-Ukraine
energy ceasefire announced by President Trump. China still
manages exports through quotas, which are likely to increase
only gradually as domestic stocks are rebuilt. Refinery runs
should lift ahead of Lunar New Year, but any increase in
exports will take time to reach regional markets.

With product supply recovering slowly and depleted inventories
being rebuilt, Singapore gasoil crack is likely to stay above its
long-run average even as Brent eases, narrowing from US$71/
bbl in Q4 to US$27/bbl by Q4 2027. With Singapore Gas 10ppm
prices, shipping and insurance costs to remain elevated and
easing only gradually in 2027, we forecast Australian diesel
to retail at an average of 274¢/L in Q4, easing to 200¢/L in Q4
2027, and petrol at 230¢/L and 187¢/L respectively.

LNG prices supported through 2027
Japanese LNG prices surged 15.2%mth in September,
averaging US$25.4/MMBtu as supply disruptions in Qatar
and its inability to divert exports around the Strait of Hormuz
tightened markets and intensified competition between
importers. While prices are now more than double pre-conflict
levels, demand destruction across Asia is providing some
offset to tight supply
Our Q4 forecast for Japanese LNG is now US$24.5/mmbtu and

we expect the market to remain structurally tight, with supply
recovery likely to be gradual and prices not returning to pre-
conflict levels until around 2028. Still, Australia’s domestic
gas market should remain insulated, supported by the FY2027
Federal Budget’s domestic gas reservation scheme, which
requires exporters to supply the domestic market with the
equivalent of up to 20% of gas exports. So far this year,
despite the increase in LNG spot prices in Asia, Australian

natural gas prices have remained under the $12 per GJ cap
that has proposed by the Commonwealth government. We are
expecting this to continue well into 2027.
Risks around our oil price outlook are two-sided
Talks are still ongoing in the background, with the US and
Iran exchanging proposals to reopen the Strait, but the gap
between them remains wide. The conflict has also expanded
to the Red Sea and, with inventories sharply drawn down,
there is a smaller buffer to absorb a further shock.

Using the Oxford Economics model, we assess an escalation
scenario, where energy markets face a deeper and more
persistent disruption as the conflict in the Middle East
intensifies. In this case, Brent futures rise to around US$150/
bbl in H1 2027, only dipping to below US$100/bbl in Q2 2028.
We also consider an early resolution scenario, where shipping
and Gulf supply normalise faster, returning Brent to near pre-
conflict levels by end-2027.
Escalation scenario

In the escalation scenario we assume:
Talks stall and the conflict escalates:  the gap between the
US and Iran proves too wide and the US resumes strikes after
the midterms. Iran responds with attacks on shipping and Gulf
energy infrastructure, damaging export, storage and refining
facilities. A lasting agreement is not reached until 2028.
Export routes are disrupted: more frequent attacks curtail
dark crossings of the Strait and ship-to-ship transfers in the

Gulf of Oman. Houthi attacks also cut off Saudi alternative
routes, forcing Gulf producers to cut output and Middle
East crude exports sharply lower. New routes and shipping
arrangements eventually support a recovery in Gulf flows, but
trade patterns take time to adjust.
... and risks remain and while they are two sided ...

### Page 4

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
4
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Product supply fails to recover:  The Russia–Ukraine energy
ceasefire does not hold and strikes on Russian refineries
continue, limiting how much diesel Russia can export,
even with the US sanctions waiver extended. China also

reintroduces its export ban to protect domestic supply.
Oil prices lift to around US$150/bbl in H1 2027: With little
buffer to absorb the supply loss, competition for physical
delivery intensifies, with physical prices well above futures
as prompt barrels remain scarce. Higher prices support
supply growth outside OPEC and drive demand destruction,
alongside further emergency stock releases, but a deeper
and more prolonged global deficit remains. Brent stays above

US$100/bbl until mid-2028 as governments rebuild reserves.
The Singapore gasoil crack widens and, with freight and
insurance costs also higher, Australian diesel and petrol prices
average around 247 cpl and 231 cpl at end 2027. Japanese
LNG also remains elevated around US$23.2/MMBtu in Q4
2027, $9.1 above our baseline.
Early resolution scenario
In the early resolution scenario we assume:
A durable agreement: The US and Iran agree terms to reopen
the Strait by the end of year, and, unlike the June interim deal,

the agreement holds, with progress towards a permanent
settlement in early 2027. Attacks on shipping cease and the
US blockade is lifted, allowing Iranian oil back into the market,
initially from storage. Vessels are quickly repositioned to the
Gulf, with freight and war-risk insurance easing from early
2027 rather than mid-year.
A faster recovery in supply:  With export routes secure, Gulf
production and damaged refinery and export capacity recover

sooner than in the baseline. Outside the Gulf, the US extends
its waiver on Russian diesel beyond April 2027 and China
raises export quotas as domestic stocks are rebuilt.
Demand settles below pre-conflict trend: Some demand
destruction and efficiency gains persist, particularly in China.
With inventories rebuilding faster, government restocking is
smaller and more gradual than in the baseline.
Prices fall quickly, but restocking provides a floor:  Brent

Brent futures ease to around US$88/bbl in Q1 2027 as the
premium for prompt physical barrels unwinds. As the market
moves into balance, Brent eases to around US$65/bbl by Q4
2027, close to pre-conflict levels.
Refined product prices ease faster on lower freight costs with
the Singapore gasoil crack narrowing. Australian diesel and
petrol prices fall to around 182 cpl and 170 cpl by Q4 2027. Gas
markets also ease, although damage to Qatar’s gas facility means

export capacity recovers gradually. Japanese LNG falls to around
US$11.9/MMBtu by end-2027, $2.1 below our baseline.
Economic impact
In the escalation scenario, higher fuel costs flow through to
transport, food and other energy-intensive goods such as
plastics and fertilisers. Australian headline CPI remains above
4.5% for much of 2027, with underlying inflation remaining
sticky at above 3% until late 2028. The RBA faces a difficult
trade off between much higher inflation and a significant

hit to activity, as households cut discretionary spending and
businesses defer investment. Higher LNG export earnings
provide only a moderate offset. In the early resolution scenario,
headline inflation is around 0.1ppt below the baseline in 2027.
New Zealand is more exposed in both scenarios, with food
and energy a larger share of its CPI and no offsetting energy
exports. Headline inflation is around 1ppt higher in the
escalation scenario in Q1 2027.

... the upside presents a more dramatic shift in prices

Document 390: September CPI_ Inflation accelerating in the run up to Christmas (2026-11-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/September CPI_ Inflation accelerating in the run up to Christmas (2026-11-01).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 18
Status: ok

### Page 1

#inflation #australia #rba #economicbulletinsaustralia #inflation
September CPI: Inflation accelerating in the run up to Christmas
By Justin Smirk & Neha Sharma
16:08 October 28 2026 Share
September Monthly CPI: Headline 0.8%mth/4.3%yr, Trimmed Mean 0.3.%mth/3.6%yr.
Market services ex volatile 1.6%mth/4.0%yr. September Quarter CPI: Headline
1.3%qtr/3.9%yr; Trimmed Mean 1.0%qtr/3.7%yr.
The CPI rose 0.8% in the September month, stronger than Westpac’s 0.6% estimate and the

market’s expectation of 0.7%. The annual pace picked up from 4.0%yr to 4.3%yr, The seasonally
adjusted CPI rose 0.6% in September highlighting some positive seasonality in the month.

### Page 2

Key factors driving the headline monthly result were: automotive fuels (9.4%mth); domestic holiday
travel & accommodation (8.0%mth); international holiday travel & accommodation (7.2%mth); and
property rates & charges (5.5%mth). There were modest offsets from: women’s garments (–
3.1%mth); household textiles (–6.6%mth); and accessories (–4.1%mth).
The Trimmed Mean (TM) gained 0.3% in September, spot on both Westpac and market expectations
with the annual pace lifting from 3.5%yr (revised from 3.6%yr) to 3.6%yr. The six-month annualised

pace is now 4.0%yr the fastest in the short history of this series.
Headline CPI lifted 1.3% in the September quarter compared to an expectation of 1.3% by Westpac
and 1.2% by the market. This saw the annual pace hold at 3.9%yr.
The RBA’s key underlying inflation measure, the quarterly TM, lifted 1.0% vs an expectation of
0.9% by Westpac and market estimate of 1.0%. The annual pace lifted from 3.6%yr to 3.7%yr
with the six-month annualised pace accelerating to 3.7%yr from 3.4%yr.

Overall, the estimates come in broadly as expected so we see minimal risk to our current December
quarter forecast of 1.3%qtr for the headline CPI. Softer housing inflation does present a very modest
downside risk to our 0.9%qtr for the TM but there are offsetting gains elsewhere.
The September CPI update revealed underlying inflationary pressures continuing even while a
moderation in housing inflation (dwellings in particular) is unfolding slightly faster than we had

anticipated. As such, the near-term risk for inflation remains to the upside. The RBA will remain alert
to these near-term inflationary risks and as so, we hold to the view that the RBA will raise rates again
at the November meeting.

### Page 3

Month of September CPI
The monthly CPI rose 0.8%mth in September, above both our expectation of a 0.6%mth increase and
the market forecast of 0.7%mth. This lifted the annual pace by 0.3ppts to 4.3%yr. On a seasonally
adjusted basis, the CPI increased 0.6%mth.
The key contributors to the monthly increase were: automotive fuel (9.4%mth, +0.3ppts); and holiday
travel & accommodation (7.6%mth, +0.5ppts), alongside a smaller contribution from the annual reset in

property rates (5.5%mth, +0.1ppt). There was no single major offset. Instead, several components
across household furnishings and clothing & footwear recorded modest declines, together subtracting a
little over 0.1ppt from the monthly result.
The monthly trimmed mean (TM) rose 0.3%mth, in line with both our and the market’s expectations.
The annual pace edged up to 3.6%yr, although the August outcome was revised down to 3.5%yr.
Underlying inflation has remained broadly stuck in the 3.5–3.6%yr range since May. On a 3m/3m basis,

the monthly TM was 1.1%.

### Page 4

Market services excluding volatile items and holiday travel were flat in the month, with annual inflation
easing to 3.8%yr, its softest pace since November 2025. On a seasonally adjusted basis, monthly
inflation moderated across most components in this measure, with telecommunications services, rents
and other financial services the key exceptions. However, all but two components are still recording
annual price growth above 3%, suggesting that disinflation across market services remains slow and

relatively narrow. The persistence of these underlying price pressures is likely to remain a concern for
the RBA.

### Page 5

Housing inflation was notably softer in the month, rising 0.3%mth to be 5.2% higher over the year.
Rents increased 0.2%mth, slightly below our expectation for a 0.3%mth rise. The biannual indexation
of Commonwealth Rent Assistance occurred late in the month, weighing on the September outcome,
with the remainder of the impact expected to flow through in October. The ABS noted that rents would
have risen 0.3%mth in the absence of the measure, while on a seasonally adjusted basis rents

increased 0.4%mth.
New dwelling inflation was flat in the month, the softest reading since May 2025. Monthly inflation has
now slowed in each successive month since May 2026, suggesting homebuilders’ pricing power is
becoming increasingly constrained. The ABS noted that some project home builders raised base prices
this month only to be offset by some builders increasing discounts and promotional offers in response
to slowing demand. The annual pace eased to 4.7%yr. Dwelling repair costs were also a downside

surprise, falling 0.1%mth, their first decline since February 2025, although prices remain 3.4% higher
over the year. Some of this softness was offset by a firmer-than-expected increase in property rates
and charges, which rose 5.6%mth.
Recently, we have also focused on audio, visual and computing given the potential for spillovers from
strong global demand for semiconductors and other computing resources associated with the AI build
out. Prices for both equipment and services came in weaker than August, with equipment prices falling

1.1%mth, while services rose 0.3%mth. Despite this, annual price growth remains relatively high for
these categories, running at 4.6% and 4.9% respectively. Some of this pressure might also be showing

### Page 6

through in gaming, toys and hobbies, which is running at 5.2%mth/15.5%yr.
Overall, the breadth of inflation moderated for a second consecutive month. The share of the basket
running above 3%yr fell from 40% to 35%, meanwhile the share below 2%yr lifted from 27% to 32%.
Our replication of the San Francisco Fed’s inflation shock momentum served as a good guide to the
quarterly TM. With the full monthly reads in for the September quarter in, this measure does signal
some persistence/upside pressures for the quarterly TM going into the coming quarters. It is worth
cautioning that this measure will be impacted as the ABS refines its seasonal adjustment process for
the monthly indexes.

Compared to our expectations, the key surprises were:
Upside surprises
Downside surprises
Fruit & vegetables was the key upside surprise, 0.6%mth against our expectations for 3.7%mth fall.
Holiday travel & accommodation also surprised to the upside, following a run of downside surprises
since early this year. It rose 7.6%mth against our expectations for a 4.4%mth lift. 
Automotive fuel rose 9.4%mth against expectations of an 8.3%mth lift.

### Page 7

September Quarter CPI
Clothing & footwear was the key downside surprise with prices falling 2.7%mth against our
expectations for a 0.2%mth fall. The misses were broad-based: garments (–2.3%mth vs –2.0%mth);
footwear (–2.5%mth vs 1.8%mth); and accessories & clothing services (–4.1%mth vs 2.8%mth).
Household textiles declined 6.6%mth against our expectations for a 3.2%mth fall. Furniture &
furnishings rose by less-than-expected 0.4%mth vs 1.6%mth. Household appliances, utensils and

tools also came in softer, –3.2%mth vs –2.4%mth.
Dairy prices fell 1.2%mth against our expectations for a 0.1%mth lift.

### Page 8

The September quarter CPI printed spot on our expectation of 1.3%. The key factor driving this
increase was the slightly stronger surge in auto fuel (7.8%, +0.27ppt) and holiday travel &
accommodation (2.7%qtr, +0.16ppt) being offset by a slightly softer than expected increase in housing
(1.1%qtr, +0.24ppt) with rents and dwellings both increasing 1.0% in the quarter and utilities lifting
1.2%. Food was also slightly stronger than expected (0.7%qtr, +0.12ppt) and while not significant to the
overall result it is interesting to track the ongoing rise in motor vehicle prices (0.3%qtr) despite a very
competitive market with Chinese manufacturers rapidly gaining market share over legacy brands.

It is worth noting that food was stronger than we expected (0.7% vs 0.5%) with a robust 1.7% increase
in restaurant meals and a solid 1.2% increase in take away & fast foods outweighing the –0.4% decline
in fresh fruit & vegetables and meat & seafoods. Overall, market services ex volatile prices increased a
very solid 1.2%qtr but with the annual pace easing to 3.2%yr from 3.36%yr.
Outside of housing the other main downside surprise in the quarter was the –0.6% fall in clothing &
footwear (+0.3% expected) with prices falling across just about all categories of garments, footwear
and accessories.
September Quarter Trimmed Mean

### Page 9

Recall that the TM is calculated from seasonally adjust consumption expenditure classes so any
numbers quoted here may not be the same as the original estimates quoted from the CPI. The TM gain
of 1.0% in the quarter was slightly stronger than we had expected even though dwellings (which have a
weight of almost 11% in the TM) was a touch softer at 1.0% compared to the 1.1% we had pencilled in.
Rents (1.0%, 10% weight) also made a solid contribution as did restaurant meals (1.7%qr, 6% weight),

take away & fast food (1.2%, 4% weight), other financial service (1.0%, 6% weight). Domestic travel
was in the TM (1.0%, 4% weight) as was medical & hospital service (1.0%, weight 5%). The range of
the TM was from 0.0% (motor vehicles) to 1.7% (beer).
What does this mean for our December quarter forecast and into 2027?
We are working though the monthly results to see what changes they may imply for individual
component inflationary momentum. Our preliminary analysis suggests that the softer trend in dwellings

is not enough, on its own, to change our Q4 CPI forecast of 1.3%. It does, however, suggest that if all
else held equal (which is not the case as there is likely to be some offsetting stronger gains elsewhere)
the moderation in dwelling inflation presents a very modest downside risk our Q4 TM estimate of 0.9%.
We are still working the numbers and will publish an updated inflation profile as soon as we can. In
addition, the overall momentum in underlying inflationary pressures suggest the downside risk to the

TM from dwellings is more likely to appear in 2027 than in the last quarter of 2026.

Document 458: 【主要貨幣攻略】升息壓力帶動美元上揚

Source PDF: all report pdf/【主要貨幣攻略】升息壓力帶動美元上揚.pdf
Report source: CTBC
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
分析師／邱日宏
研究範疇／美元 /歐元 /日圓 /英鎊 /瑞郎 /台幣
主要貨幣投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/10/1
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《通膨帶動升息壓力，美元走強》
美元從2026年初降息交易轉向受到美伊戰爭 、油價
上漲及通膨預期升溫支撐，但此波強勢主要來自地緣
政治風險及利率維持高檔 。從9月Fed季度經濟預測
可見，經濟成長展望維持在 2.5%以下溫和成長 ，通
膨中長期則預期回穩但2026可能繼續維持在3%以上，
因此4Q26美國貨幣政策將繼續有升息的壓力 。從聯
準會點陣圖及 ECB後續政策路徑觀察 ，市場對歐美
利差擴大的預期對美元有利。然而，歐日景氣動能

3
《歐元投資關鍵-歐元區經濟活動加速擴張》
7月以來，北約示警灰色地帶模糊戰的風險升溫，導
致歐元短線偏弱 ，歐洲資金明顯轉向瑞郎進行避險 。
然而，歐洲基本面此時卻持續改善。歐元區9月景氣
加速擴張 ，綜合PMI初值創41個月新高 ，服務業與
製造業同步改善 ，其中製造業產出創 55個月高點。
德國成長動能增強 ，法國則10個月來首度恢復擴張 。
新訂單連續第3個月成長，增速為2022年5月以來最
快，出口訂單亦持續回升，帶動未完成訂單自2022
年6月以來首度增加。另一方面，中東衝突推升能源

價格，使投入成本與售價增速升至近4個月高點。整
體而言，歐元區經濟韌性優於預期 ，但通膨升溫與
企業信心偏弱仍是後續關注焦點。
貨幣政策方面 ，歐洲央行官員持續釋出偏鷹訊號 ，
認為能源價格再度上漲將推升並延長通膨壓力 ，未
來數月歐元區通膨可能接近4%。愛爾蘭央行行長表
示不排除10月再度升息，強調每次會議皆有行動可
能。首席經濟學家 Lane指出第 2輪能源衝擊將帶動
食品與商品價格上漲 。多位官員認為經濟仍具韌性 ，
為進一步收緊政策提供空間 ，市場也預期未來一年
歐洲央行仍有多次升息機會 ，以防止通膨預期根深
蒂固。基本面繼續支持 ECB升息的環境下 ，預計美
歐利差收斂將提供歐元支撐。4Q26目標價1.19。

4
《英鎊投資關鍵-英國央行維持利率3.75%不變》
9月經濟數據方面，9月PMI連續第3個月擴張，但成
長動能放緩。綜合PMI初值創6月以來新低，製造業
與服務業活動同步降溫 。其中，服務業受內需疲弱
及地緣政治不確定性拖累；製造業則受惠於 AI投資
與國防支出增加 ，但消費需求疲弱仍構成壓力 。通
膨壓力持續升溫，企業投入成本創6月以來最高，並
推升企業售價增幅 。英國經濟維持低速擴張 ，GDP
季增率僅約0.1%，但成本與通膨壓力升高 ，反映經
濟成長疲弱與通膨升溫並存的局面。
貨幣政策方面，9月央行維持利率3.75%不變，為成

熟國家少數維持利率不變的國家，英鎊兌美元創3個
月新低。央行總裁貝里表示 ，能源成本上升對英國
物價和薪資影響有限 ，但若這種波動持續下去 ，未
來有可能需要調高基準利率 。目前消費者物價指數
為3.1%，低於4月央行預測值 ，實際通膨展望低於
預期提供央行維持貨幣政策的基調 。展望後續，歐
非中東地緣政治情勢短期仍處於高不確定性階段 ，
並且導致近期天然氣價格飆升 ，英國仍有升息壓力 。
英國貨幣政策短線持穩 ，而經濟數據面則小幅改善 ，
但財政政策不確定性風險大幅削弱英鎊的投資誘因 ，
維持英鎊中性評等，英鎊兌美元年底目標1.34。

後大幅拉回 ，2026年7-8月份日本干預匯市共動用
15.4兆日圓，創2022年以來最高金額。8月美日聯手

最大養老基金)進場購買日本公債。同時，美國財長
貝森特示警日圓空頭交易，表示｢掌握不對稱資訊，
現在莊家是我｣。加上日本公布7月名目現金薪資年增
4.7%，創29年最大升幅，日本央行貨幣政策正常化
路徑延續。貝森特支持日本以美國公債作為抵押，讓

日本在不出售美國公債的情況下買入日圓 (FIMA-外
國與國際貨幣當局附買回機制)。目前日本持有1.1兆
美元公債，同時加上美國政府表態支持干預弱勢日圓，
美日聯手讓日圓短線貶勢暫時告一段落。美元兌日圓
9月在160再度形成一波回檔，顯示美日政府力守160
整點關卡的決心。目前日本利率(無擔保隔夜拆款利
率)1.25%水準再同時加入日圓隔夜指數交換利率隱
含升息3碼，2027年利率水準與歐美差距仍有一段距
離，實質利率仍為負值，且在成熟市場中偏低。除此
之外，高市政府擴張性財政政策及新增支出計畫恐持
續不利日圓。高市於7月30日宣布自2027年4月起，
將現行8%食品消費稅率降至1%，實施期限為2年，

Document 517: フランス国債売りは行きすぎか？ ～格下げ回避で一息も、政権交代後の財政運営に潜むリスク～

Source PDF: all report pdf/フランス国債売りは行きすぎか？ ～格下げ回避で一息も、政権交代後の財政運営に潜むリスク～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Europe Trends

2026 年10 月 26 日

1/9 
フランス国債売りは行きすぎか？ 
～格下げ回避で一息も、政権交代後の財政運営に潜むリスク～ 
 経済調査部 首席エコノミスト  田中 理 
（℡：050-5474-7494）

（要旨） 
○ 米格付け会社ムーディーズは23 日、フランス国債の格下げを見送ったが、 財政再建の難航や
政治・社会情勢の混乱に対する懸念は解消していない。国債市場では、財政悪化や政治的な
機能不全の長期化に加え、2027 年大統領選挙での極右・国民連合（ＲＮ）の勝利も相当程度
織り込まれつつある。むしろ重要なのは、政権交代後も一定の財政規律が維持されるかどう
かであろう。ＲＮが政権を獲得した場合、議会の勢力図次第で現実路線への転換が進む可能
性がある一方、政治的な行き詰まりや財政拡張、欧州連合（ＥＵ）との対立が深まる恐れも
ある。格下げ回避や抗議活動の暴力行為の減少を受け、目先は国債の買い戻しが進む余地が
あるものの、予算審議の難航や根強い社会不安が重石となる。財政再建に向けた政治的な合
意が形成されない限り、 国債利回りの高止まりと相場の不安定化が長期化すると考えられる。

■ムーディーズが格下げ見送り、財政再建への警戒は解けず 
米格付け会社ムーディーズは10 月23 日、フランス国債の格付けに関する定期審査の結果を公
表し、格下げを見送った。長期国債格付けは「Ａａ３」 、見通しは「ネガティブ」に維持された
（図表１）。金融市場では、財政再建の難航や政治・社会情勢の混乱を受け、格下げを予想する市
場参加者が多かっただけに、予想外の結果となった。ただ、今回の公表は正式な格付け変更を伴
わない定期審査であり、財政・政治リスクに対する厳しい評価が変わった訳ではない。ムーディ
ーズは、豊かで多様化した経済、高い民間貯蓄、質の高い行政機構をフランスの信用力を支える
要因として評価する一方、政治的な分断と社会不安が財政再建を妨げることに警戒を示した。 
今回の審査では、2027 年の財政赤字が政府目標の国内総生産（ＧＤＰ）比5.0％を上回る5.2％
になると予想し、政府債務残高は2027 年にＧＤＰ比 120％を超え、その後も増加が続くと見込ん
でいる。そのうえで、財政赤字の削減措置を盛り込んだ2027 年予算が最終的に成立し、国債の調
達コストと社会的な緊張が今後数カ月で幾分和らぐことを格付け維持の前提としている。また、
2027 年春の大統領選挙後も一定の財政再建努力が続くことを想定する一方、政治的な分断、社会
的な緊張、低成長によって急速な財政再建は難しいと判断している。ムーディーズは、議会の政
策決定能力が一段と低下する場合や、2023 年の年金改革の主要部分が長期にわたって停止・撤回
される場合には、格下げ圧力が高まると警告している。

### Page 2

2/9

こうしたなか、24 日には前週に続いて全国各地で大規模な抗議活動が行われた。内務省による
と、全国で約 41 万人が参加し、17 日の55 万人から減少したが、今回も一部の都市で警察との衝
突や商店の略奪が発生し、全国で1,314 人が拘束された。ただ、前週の2,628 人から拘束者数は
半減し、略奪や放火などの被害も大幅に減少した。事前の警備強化や労働組合による行進の管理
が奏功し、17 日のような大規模な暴動の再発はひとまず回避された。もっとも、11 月５日には労
働組合が全国的なストライキと抗議活動を予定しており、社会不安が収束したとは言い難い。 
一方、予算審議は早くも難航している。国民議会は23 日、2027 年社会保障予算案の歳入部分を
賛成１票、反対115 票の圧倒的多数で否決した。これにより、歳出部分の審議に進むことができ
ず、法案全体が下院の第一読会で否決された。国家予算案についても、当初20 日に予定されてい
た歳入部分の採決が延期されるなど、審議は遅れている。格下げこそ回避したが、財政再建に必
要な予算の成立には不透明感が残る。ムーディーズが格付け維持の前提とする財政再建努力の継
続と社会的緊張の緩和が実現するかどうか、今後の政治情勢が改めて問われることになる。

### Page 3

■市場は既に深刻な財政悪化と政治的混乱を織り込む 
フランスの国債利回りはこのところ急速に上昇してきた。10 年物国債利回りは５％を超え、
2002 年以来の高水準にある。ユーロ圏の安全資産であるドイツ国債との利回り格差（スプレッ
ド）は、９月初めの100bp前後から170 bp前後に急拡大し、2011 年の欧州債務危機以来の水準に
達している。世界的な長期金利の上昇に加え、フランス固有の財政・政治リスクが国債売りに拍
車をかけている。 
それでは、金融市場はどこまでの悪材料を織り込んでいるのか。まず、2027 年の財政赤字削減
目標が達成されず、政府債務比率の上昇が続くことについては、相当程度織り込みが進んでいる
と考えられる。今回の格下げは見送られたものの、政治的な分断が財政再建を妨げ、国債の格下
げが続く可能性もある程度は国債価格に反映されているとみられる。現在のフランス国債利回り
は、格付けがより低いイタリアやギリシャの国債利回りを上回っている（図表２）。これは、投資
家が信用格付けだけでは説明できない政治的な不確実性の高さや財政運営の実行能力に対して、
高い上乗せ金利を要求していることを示している。

### Page 4

4/9 
もっとも、最近の独仏スプレッド拡大の全てを、フランスの債務不履行リスクの高まりと解釈
するのは適切ではない。フランス国債の対独スプレッドと、信用リスクを反映するクレジット・
デフォルト・スワップ（ＣＤＳ）の信用保証料の乖離が大きく拡大している。こうした乖離は、
財政の持続可能性への懸念に加え、政治的な不透明感、国債増発に伴う需給悪化、投資家のリス
ク回避姿勢など、債務不履行リスク以外の要因によるプレミアムが拡大していると考えられる。 
現在の市場は、フランス財政の悪化や政治的な機能不全が長期化することを相当程度織り込む
一方、政府が国債市場から安定的に資金調達できなくなるような深刻な信用危機までは、中心シ
ナリオとして織り込んでいないとみられる。2010 年代初めの欧州債務危機では、ギリシャやアイ
ルランド、ポルトガルの10 年物国債利回りが７％を超えた後、自力での国債調達が困難となり、
欧州連合（ＥＵ）や国際通貨基金（ＩＭＦ）による財政支援を要請した。７％は危機発生の明確
な境界線ではなく、財政の持続可能性を左右する金利水準は各国の債務構造や経済成長率などに
よって異なる。フランスは経済規模が大きく、国債市場の流動性も高い。国債の平均残存年限も
約 8.5 年と長く、市場金利の急上昇が直ちに政府債務全体の利払い負担を大きく押し上げるわけ
ではない。目先の金利上昇が短期間で財政破綻につながると考える必要はないが、高金利が長期
化すれば、借り換えを通じて財政負担は徐々に増していく。

■ルペン政権の誕生も意識する市場、財政再建路線の継続を前提に 
今後の国債市場を展望するうえで、2027 年春の大統領選挙も重要な論点となる。各種の世論調
査では、極右政党・国民連合（ＲＮ）のルペン氏が30％以上の支持を集め、他候補を大きくリー
ドしている。極左政党・不服従のフランス（ＬＦＩ）のメランション氏、社会党（ＰＳ）など中
道左派勢力の予備選挙に勝利したグリュックスマン氏、中道政党・地平線（オリゾン）のフィリ
ップ氏が10％台半ばで熾烈な二番手争いを繰り広げ、その後を、マクロン大統領の中道政党・ル
ネサンスのアタル氏、中道右派・共和党（ＬＲ）のルタイヨ氏などが追う（図表３）。フランスの
大統領選挙は二回投票制で行われ、初回投票で過半数を確保した候補がいない場合、上位２名が
決選投票に進出し、そこでの勝者が大統領に就任する。 
しばらく前までは、中道勢力がフィリップ氏に一本化した場合、メランション氏に競り勝ち、
二番手で決選投票に進出する世論調査が多かった。ところが、最近では中道がフィリップ氏に一
本化した場合もメランション氏との二番手争いに敗れ、決選投票への進出を逃す調査が増えてい
る。決選投票の組み合わせ別の世論調査では、フィリップ氏やグリュックスマン氏がルペン氏と
対峙した場合はルペン氏がやや優勢である一方、メランション氏との対決ではルペン氏が大差で
勝利する調査が多い（図表４）。つまり、最近の世論調査は、ルペン氏とメランション氏の決選投
票への進出と、決選投票でルペン氏が勝利するシナリオの現実味が高まっていることを示唆して
いる。ルペン氏の勝利はもはや、金融市場にとって想定外の事態とは言えない。

### Page 6

6/9

むしろ、国債市場にとって重要なのは政権交代後の財政運営である。ルペン氏は10 月６日、
2027 年大統領選挙に向けた独自の財政再建計画を発表した。政権発足後18 カ月以内に利払い費を
除く基礎的財政収支を均衡させ、2030 年までに財政赤字をＧＤＰ比で３％未満に縮小し、2032 年
までに政府債務比率を112％程度に引き下げることを目指す。同時に、300 億ユーロ規模の減税を
行うとしており、移民関連支出や社会保障支出、行政コストの削減などを通じて、2032 年までに
2026 年対比で年間1,400 億ユーロ規模の歳出削減を実現するとしている。また、財政規律を強制
力のある憲法上の「黄金律」として定めることも提案した。具体的には、政府債務残高のＧＤＰ
比率が毎年低下するよう、経済成長率や債務残高に応じて財政赤字の上限を設定し、政府や議会
にその遵守を義務付ける仕組みである。国民投票を通じて憲法に明記することで、政権交代後も
財政規律を維持する制度的な枠組みを構築し、金融市場の信認回復につなげる狙いがある。 
ただし、ＲＮの財政再建計画の実現可能性には疑問が残る。移民関連支出や行政コストの削減
には限界があり、ＥＵ予算への拠出金削減など、フランス政府の一存では実現できない措置も含
まれる。一方、ＲＮは従来から年金改革の見直しや家計の購買力改善など、財政負担を伴う政策
も掲げてきた。こうした政策と大規模な歳出削減を両立させることは容易ではない。 
それでも、ルペン氏が財政再建を重視する姿勢を示していることには意味がある。政権獲得後
に国債市場の信認を失えば、政府の資金調達コストが上昇し、公約の実現や安定した政権運営が
難しくなる。イタリアでも、過去にＥＵの財政規律に批判的だったメローニ首相が、政権発足後
は金融市場やＥＵとの関係に配慮した財政運営を進めてきた。フランスでも同様に、政権獲得後
に現実路線へ移行する可能性を市場参加者が意識していると考えられる。 
このため、現在のフランス国債市場は、ルペン氏の大統領選挙の勝利を相当程度意識しつつ、
独自の財政再建計画が完全には実現しなくても、一定の財政規律が維持されることを前提として
いるとみられる。ムーディーズも、次期大統領と政府が財政赤字削減を継続するとの基本シナリ
オを示している。市場が十分に織り込んでいないと考えられるのは、政権交代そのものよりも、
次期政権が財政再建路線を事実上放棄し、財源の裏付けがない財政拡張に転じる展開であろう。

■大統領選挙後の議会選挙が政権運営の分岐点に 
ただ、ルペン氏が大統領選挙で勝利したとしても、ＲＮが安定した政権運営を実現できるとは
限らない。フランスでは過去に、大統領と国民議会（下院）の任期がずれていたことから、大統
領の出身政党と議会の多数派が食い違う「ねじれ（コアビタシオン） 」が生じ、政権運営が混乱し
た。2000 年の憲法改正によって大統領任期が７年から５年に短縮され、2002 年以降は大統領選挙
の直後に国民議会選挙を実施する日程が定着したことで、ねじれが生じにくくなった。ところ
が、2024 年の議会解散によって選挙サイクルがずれており、次の議会任期満了は2029 年となる。
現在の議会は、左派勢力、中道勢力、極右勢力の間で議席が割れ、何れの勢力も議会の過半数を
確保できていない。2027 年の大統領選挙で新大統領が就任した場合、安定した議会基盤を確保す
るため、直後に議会を解散し、前倒しで国民議会選挙を行う可能性が高い。

### Page 7

7/9 
仮に解散・総選挙が実施されても、ＲＮが議会の過半数を獲得できるとは限らない。フランス
の国民議会選挙は小選挙区・２回投票制で行われ、初回投票で高い支持を集めても、決選投票で
他党の候補者に敗れるケースがある。2024 年の議会選挙では、ＲＮへの反発から中道勢力と左派
勢力が候補者を調整した結果、ＲＮの議席が伸び悩んだ。大統領選挙での勝利がＲＮへの支持を
さらに広げる可能性がある一方、反ＲＮ勢力による候補者調整や戦術投票が再び行われることも
考えられる。したがって、ＲＮが大統領選挙に勝利した場合でも、議会で単独過半数を獲得する
場合と、過半数を欠く場合の双方を想定する必要がある。 
ＲＮが過半数を確保できなかった場合、その後の政権運営は大きく二つの方向に分かれる。一
つは、中道や中道右派勢力との協力を模索し、財政規律やＥＵとの協調を重視する現実路線に移
行するシナリオである。ＲＮとの協力を拒む勢力も多いため、正式な連立政権の形成は容易では
ないが、個別の予算や法案ごとに他党の支持を取り付けることは考えられる。その際、年金改革
の全面的な撤回や大規模な減税など、財政負担を伴う政策の軌道修正を求められる可能性があ
る。中道勢力との政策協調が実現すれば、政権交代後の財政運営に対する市場の警戒が和らぐと
みられる。 
 もう一つは、他党との協力が進まず、政権運営が行き詰まるシナリオである。財政再建に必要
な政策が議会で成立せず、支持層の期待に応えることも難しくなれば、政府が政治的な対立を強
める可能性がある。特にＲＮは従来からＥＵの政策に批判的な姿勢を示してきたため、国内の政
策運営が行き詰まった場合に、ＥＵの財政規律や予算拠出金などへの批判を強め、欧州委員会と
の対立が深まる展開も考えられる。この場合、財政再建の停滞に加えて、ＥＵとの関係悪化がフ
ランスの国債市場の新たな不安材料となりかねない。 
さらに、ＲＮが議会の過半数を獲得した場合にも、財政運営に対する不安が広がる恐れがあ
る。議会での政策実行力が高まることで、他党との政策調整を必要としなくなるため、年金改革
の撤回や減税など、支持層への公約履行を優先する誘因が強まることが考えられる。歳出削減の
計画が予定通りに実現せず、財源の裏付けが乏しい支出拡大が先行すれば、政権が強固な議会基
盤を持つことが、必ずしも国債市場の安心材料になるとは限らない。 
金融市場にとって重要なのは、単にＲＮが議会の過半数を確保できるかどうかではなく、議会
の勢力図が財政規律の維持と政策実行能力にどのような影響を与えるかである。ＲＮが過半数を
欠いた場合には、中道勢力との協力によって現実路線に移行する可能性がある一方、ＲＮとの協
力に対する中道勢力の抵抗が根強ければ、予算や重要法案の成立が困難となり、現政権と同様の
政治的な機能不全が続く恐れもある。財政規律を重視する姿勢を掲げていても、それを実行に移
す議会基盤を欠く場合、市場の信認回復にはつながりにくい。反対に、ＲＮが過半数を確保した
場合には政策実行力が高まるものの、財政負担を伴う公約の実現を優先するリスクが残る。大統
領選挙後の議会勢力図と、それに伴う政策運営の方向性が、フランス国債市場の重要な分岐点に
なると考えられる。

■格下げ回避で目先の買い戻し余地も、国債相場の不安定化は長期化

### Page 8

8/9
今後のフランスの国債相場を展望すると、ムーディーズによる格下げ見送りを受けて、目先は
行き過ぎた国債売りの巻き戻しが進む可能性がある。独仏スプレッドは170bpを超え、政治的な不
透明感や市場の需給悪化を相当程度反映した水準に達している。格下げが警戒されていただけ
に、短期的には国債を買い戻す動きが強まる余地がある。ただし、格付け見通しはネガティブの
ままであり、財政再建の難航や社会不安が解消したわけではない。予算審議の進展や抗議活動の
沈静化が確認されない限り、国債相場の本格的な回復は見込みにくい。
当面の注目点は、2027 年予算の成立に向けた議会審議と、社会不安の行方である。予算審議で
は、政府がどこまで野党の修正要求を受け入れ、財政赤字の削減目標と政権維持を両立できるか
が焦点となる。政府が年内成立を優先して歳出削減や増税措置を大幅に後退させれば、たとえ予
算が成立しても財政再建の実効性に疑問が残る。他方、政府が財政規律を重視する結果、野党と
の対立を深めれば、予算の不成立や内閣不信任につながる恐れがある。重要なのは、予算が成立

するか否かだけではなく、最終的にどの程度の財政改善が確保され、政府債務比率の安定化につ
ながるかである。
また、11 月５日に予定される全国的なストライキと抗議活動も注視する必要がある。24 日の抗
議活動では17 日のような大規模な暴動が回避されたものの、参加者数は依然として高水準にあ
る。今後、抗議活動が政府の予算修正や政策変更を促す方向に進むのか、それとも一部の暴力行
為を伴いながら長期化し、政権の政策実行能力を一段と低下させるのかが重要となる。特に、年
金改革の撤回や社会保障支出の削減見送りなど、恒久的な歳出増加につながる譲歩が拡大する場
合には、財政再建の遅れに対する市場の警戒が強まる可能性がある。
さらに、金融市場では、独仏スプレッドの水準そのものだけでなく、他のユーロ圏国債への波
及状況や、フランス国債の信用リスク指標の動向を見極める必要がある。フランス国債の利回り
が上昇しても、対独スプレッドの拡大が中心で、ＣＤＳの信用保証料や他国の国債利回りが比較
的安定している場合には、政治・財政リスクに伴うプレミアムの拡大と解釈できる。一方、ＣＤ

Ｓの信用保証料が急上昇し、イタリアやベルギーなどの国債市場にも売り圧力が波及する場合に
は、フランス固有の財政不安がユーロ圏全体の金融市場の安定性を脅かす段階に移行する恐れが
高まる。そうした局面では、欧州中央銀行（ＥＣＢ）の伝達保護措置（ＴＰＩ）など、市場安定
化に向けた対応が改めて注目される。ただし、ＴＰＩは健全なマクロ経済・財政政策などを適格
性の判断要素としているため、財政再建を巡る政治的な行き詰まりが長引くフランスに対して、
無条件に発動できるわけではない。
今後の国債市場には、大きく三つのシナリオが考えられる。第一は、2027 年予算が成立し、社
会不安も沈静化することで、独仏スプレッドが縮小するシナリオである。第二は、予算は成立す
るものの財政再建が限定的にとどまり、政府債務比率の上昇が続くことで、スプレッドが高水準
で推移するシナリオである。第三は、予算審議の頓挫や政権交代後の財政規律の放棄、ＥＵとの
対立深刻化などを受けて、国債市場の信認がさらに損なわれるシナリオである。特に三番目のケ

ースでは、フランスに対するリスクプレミアムが一段と拡大し、国債利回りが現在の水準からさ
らに大幅に上昇する可能性がある。

### Page 9

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
9/9 
現時点で注目すべきは、フランスが直ちに財政破綻するかどうかではない。財政悪化と政治的
な混乱が長期化することは既に国債市場に相当程度織り込まれている一方、財政再建の取り組み
自体が放棄される展開までは十分に織り込まれていないと考えられる。24 日の抗議活動では前週
のような大規模な暴動は回避され、ムーディーズによる格下げ見送りとともに、目先の国債相場
に一定の安心材料を提供する可能性がある。ただし、社会不安は依然として根強く、予算審議も
難航している。政府が社会的な反発を抑えながら財政再建を進めることができるかについては、
依然として不透明感が強い。 
ムーディーズの格下げ見送りは、フランスの財政・政治リスクが解消したことを意味するもの
ではない。むしろ、今後の予算審議や抗議活動の行方を通じて、政府の政策実行能力が改めて試
されることになる。さらに、2027 年の大統領選挙ではＲＮの政権獲得が現実味を帯びており、金
融市場は政権交代そのものよりも、次期政権が財政規律を維持できるかに注目している。ＲＮが
中道勢力との協力によって現実路線に移行するのか、それとも政権運営の行き詰まりからＥＵと
の対立を深めるのかが、国債相場の重要な分岐点となろう。短期的には行き過ぎた国債売りの巻
き戻しが進む余地があるものの、財政再建に向けた政治的な合意が形成されない限り、国債利回
りの高止まりと相場の不安定な動きが長期化する可能性が高い。

以 上

### Page 1

米国

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番 1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー
